    ${html}
    `; } const EPIC_BOLT = ''; const epicNewBtn = (label, onclick, extra) => ``; function qrMatrix(text, forceMask) { const data = Array.from(new TextEncoder().encode(String(text))); const SPEC = [null, [16, 10, 1], [28, 16, 1], [44, 26, 1], [64, 18, 2], [86, 24, 2], [108, 16, 4]]; let ver = 1; while (ver <= 6 && data.length + 2 > SPEC[ver][0]) ver++; if (ver > 6) throw new Error('That text is too long for a QR label'); const [dcw, ecw, nb] = SPEC[ver], size = ver * 4 + 17; const bits = []; const put = (v, n) => { for (let i = n - 1; i >= 0; i--) bits.push((v >> i) & 1); }; put(4, 4); put(data.length, 8); data.forEach(b => put(b, 8)); for (let i = 0; i < 4 && bits.length < dcw * 8; i++) bits.push(0); while (bits.length % 8) bits.push(0); const cw = []; for (let i = 0; i < bits.length; i += 8) cw.push(parseInt(bits.slice(i, i + 8).join(''), 2)); for (let p = 0; cw.length < dcw; p ^= 1) cw.push(p ? 0x11 : 0xec); const EXP = new Array(512), LOG = new Array(256); for (let i = 0, x = 1; i < 255; i++) { EXP[i] = x; LOG[x] = i; x <<= 1; if (x & 256) x ^= 0x11d; } for (let i = 255; i < 512; i++) EXP[i] = EXP[i - 255]; const mul = (a, b) => a && b ? EXP[LOG[a] + LOG[b]] : 0; let gen = [1]; for (let i = 0; i < ecw; i++) { const g = new Array(gen.length + 1).fill(0); gen.forEach((c, j) => { g[j] ^= c; g[j + 1] ^= mul(c, EXP[i]); }); gen = g; } const bl = dcw / nb, blocks = [], ecs = []; for (let b = 0; b < nb; b++) { const d = cw.slice(b * bl, b * bl + bl), r = d.concat(new Array(ecw).fill(0)); for (let i = 0; i < d.length; i++) { const f = r[i]; if (f) for (let j = 1; j < gen.length; j++) r[i + j] ^= mul(gen[j], f); } blocks.push(d); ecs.push(r.slice(d.length)); } const all = []; for (let i = 0; i < bl; i++) blocks.forEach(d => all.push(d[i])); for (let i = 0; i < ecw; i++) ecs.forEach(e => all.push(e[i])); const M = Array.from({ length: size }, () => new Array(size).fill(null)), fixed = Array.from({ length: size }, () => new Array(size).fill(false)); const set = (r, c, v) => { if (r >= 0 && c >= 0 && r < size && c < size) { M[r][c] = v; fixed[r][c] = true; } }; const finder = (r0, c0) => { for (let r = -1; r <= 7; r++) for (let c = -1; c <= 7; c++) set(r0 + r, c0 + c, r >= 0 && r <= 6 && c >= 0 && c <= 6 && (r === 0 || r === 6 || c === 0 || c === 6 || (r >= 2 && r <= 4 && c >= 2 && c <= 4))); }; finder(0, 0); finder(0, size - 7); finder(size - 7, 0); for (let i = 8; i < size - 8; i++) { set(6, i, i % 2 === 0); set(i, 6, i % 2 === 0); } if (ver > 1) { const p = [6, ver * 4 + 10]; p.forEach(r => p.forEach(c => { if (fixed[r][c]) return; for (let y = -2; y <= 2; y++) for (let x = -2; x <= 2; x++) set(r + y, c + x, Math.max(Math.abs(y), Math.abs(x)) !== 1); })); } for (let i = 0; i < 9; i++) { if (!fixed[8][i]) set(8, i, false); if (!fixed[i][8]) set(i, 8, false); } for (let i = 0; i < 8; i++) { set(8, size - 1 - i, false); if (i < 7) set(size - 1 - i, 8, false); } set(size - 8, 8, true); const dbits = []; all.forEach(b => { for (let i = 7; i >= 0; i--) dbits.push((b >> i) & 1); }); let k = 0; for (let c = size - 1; c > 0; c -= 2) { if (c === 6) c--; for (let n = 0; n < size; n++) { const r = ((c + 1) & 2) ? n : size - 1 - n; for (let d = 0; d < 2; d++) { const cc = c - d; if (!fixed[r][cc]) M[r][cc] = !!dbits[k++]; } } } const MASKS = [(r, c) => (r + c) % 2 === 0, r => r % 2 === 0, (r, c) => c % 3 === 0, (r, c) => (r + c) % 3 === 0, (r, c) => (Math.floor(r / 2) + Math.floor(c / 3)) % 2 === 0, (r, c) => (r * c) % 2 + (r * c) % 3 === 0, (r, c) => ((r * c) % 2 + (r * c) % 3) % 2 === 0, (r, c) => ((r + c) % 2 + (r * c) % 3) % 2 === 0]; const build = m => { const G = M.map((row, r) => row.map((v, c) => fixed[r][c] ? v : v !== MASKS[m](r, c))); let f = (0 << 3 | m) << 10; for (let i = 14; i >= 10; i--) if (f >> i & 1) f ^= 0x537 << (i - 10); f = ((0 << 3 | m) << 10 | f) ^ 0x5412; const fb = i => !!((f >> i) & 1); for (let i = 0; i <= 5; i++) G[i][8] = fb(i); G[7][8] = fb(6); G[8][8] = fb(7); G[8][7] = fb(8); for (let i = 9; i < 15; i++) G[8][14 - i] = fb(i); for (let i = 0; i < 8; i++) G[8][size - 1 - i] = fb(i); for (let i = 8; i < 15; i++) G[size - 15 + i][8] = fb(i); return G; }; const penalty = G => { let p = 0; const n = size; for (let pass = 0; pass < 2; pass++) for (let a = 0; a < n; a++) { let run = 1; for (let b = 1; b < n; b++) { const x = pass ? G[b][a] : G[a][b], y = pass ? G[b - 1][a] : G[a][b - 1]; if (x === y) { run++; if (run === 5) p += 3; else if (run > 5) p++; } else run = 1; } } for (let r = 0; r < n - 1; r++) for (let c = 0; c < n - 1; c++) { const v = G[r][c]; if (v === G[r][c + 1] && v === G[r + 1][c] && v === G[r + 1][c + 1]) p += 3; } const pat = [1, 0, 1, 1, 1, 0, 1, 0, 0, 0, 0], pat2 = pat.slice().reverse(); for (let a = 0; a < n; a++) for (let b = 0; b <= n - 11; b++) [pat, pat2].forEach(P => { if (P.every((v, i) => !!v === G[a][b + i])) p += 40; if (P.every((v, i) => !!v === G[b + i][a])) p += 40; }); let dark = 0; G.forEach(row => row.forEach(v => { if (v) dark++; })); p += Math.floor(Math.abs(dark * 20 - n * n * 10) / (n * n)) * 10; return p; }; if (forceMask != null) return build(forceMask); let best = null, bp = Infinity; for (let m = 0; m < 8; m++) { const G = build(m), q = penalty(G); if (q < bp) { bp = q; best = G; } } return best; } function qrSvg(text, px, quiet) { const m = qrMatrix(text), q = quiet == null ? (\d{4}))?$/.exec(q))) { const mi = MONTHS.indexOf(m[1].slice(0, 3)); const full = mi >= 0 && new Date(2000, mi, 1).toLocaleDateString('en-US', { month: 'long' }).toLowerCase(); if (mi < 0 || !full.startsWith(m[1]) || (m[1] === 'may' ? false : m[1].length < 3)) return null; return { m: mi + 1, d: m[2] ? +m[2] : null, y: m[3] ? +m[3] : null }; } if ((m = /^(\d{1,2})\/(\d{1,2})(?:\/(\d{2}|\d{4}))?$/.exec(q))) return { m: +m[1], d: +m[2], y: m[3] ?     '; } function activeFilters(kind) { const q = $('search') ? $('search').value.trim() : '', out = []; if (q) out.push(['q', `🔍 “${q}”`]); if (kind === 'item') { if (locFilter) out.push(['loc', '📍 ' + locFilter]); if (catFilter.size) { const labels = categories().filter(c => catFilter.has(c.key)).map(c => c.label); out.push(['cat', '🗂 ' + (labels.length === 1 ? labels[0] : labels.length + ' categories')]); } if (lowFilter) out.push(['low', '📉 Low stock']); if (qtyColors.size) out.push(['color', '🎨 ' + (qtyColors.size === 1 ? labelOf([...qtyColors][0]) : qtyColors.size + ' labels')]); if (searchOut.size && q) out.push(['out', `✕ ${searchOut.size} removed`]); } else { if (orngOn()) out.push(['range', '📅 ' + orngLabel()]); if (orderEp.sel.size) out.push(['oep', `🔌 ${orderEp.sel.size} endpoint${orderEp.sel.size === 1 ?     Loading contacts…
    '); $('sheet').dataset.panel = 'contacts'; Promise.all([loadContacts(), ordersLoaded ? null : loadOrders()]).then(() => { if ($('sheet').dataset.panel === 'contacts') renderContacts(); }); } function contactDupGroups() { const groups = [], seen = new Set(); contacts.forEach(c => { if (seen.has(c.id)) return; const g = [c].concat(contacts.filter(o => o.id !== c.id && !seen.has(o.id) && sameContact(c, o))); if (g.length > 1) { g.forEach(x => seen.add(x.id)); groups.push(g); } }); return groups; } function sameContact(a, b) { const em = x => String(x.email || '').trim().toLowerCase(); const an = contactKey(a.name), bn = contactKey(b.name), ac = contactKey(a.company), bc = contactKey(b.company); return (em(a) && em(a) === em(b)) || (contactPhone(a.phone) && contactPhone(a.phone) === contactPhone(b.phone)) || ((an || ac) && an === bn && ac === bc) || (ac && ac === bc && (!an || !bn)) || (an && an === bn && (!ac || !bc)); } function contactsFromOrders() { const fields = [['contact', 'Point of Contact', 'Other'], ['vendor', 'Company / Vendor', 'Vendor'], ['freight', 'Freight', 'Freight / Carrier'], ['pm', 'PM', 'PM']]; const found = {}; orders.forEach(o => fields.forEach(([f, label, type]) => { const t = String(o[f] || '').trim(), k = contactKey(t); if (!k || contactFor(t, f)) return; const e = found[f + '|' + k] || (found[f + '|' + k] = { text: t, field: f, label, type, count: 0 }); e.count++; })); return Object.values(found).sort((a, b) => b.count - a.count || a.text.localeCompare(b.text)); } function renderContacts() { if (!contactsLoaded) return; const q = norm(ct.q), dups = contactDupGroups(), fromOrders = canEdit() ? contactsFromOrders() : []; const counts = {}; contacts.forEach(c => counts[c.type] = (counts[c.type] || 0) + 1); const list = contacts.filter(c => (!ct.type || c.type === ct.type) && (!q || norm([c.name, c.company, c.title, c.phone, c.email, c.address, c.notes, c.type].join(' ')).includes(q))); const tabs = `

    `); $('sheet').dataset.panel = 'contacts'; $('sheet').scrollTop = top; if ($('ctQ') && document.activeElement !== $('ctQ') && ct.q) { $('ctQ').focus(); $('ctQ').setSelectionRange(ct.q.length, ct.q.length); } } function contactListHtml(list) { if (!contacts.length) return `
    No contacts yet.${canEdit() ? '
    Tap ＋ New contact, or use 📥 From orders to add the names already on your orders.' : ''}
    `; if (!list.length) return '
    No contacts match.

    `).join(''); function renderTagBox(field, refocus) { const F = TAG_FIELDS[field]; if (!$(F.box)) return; $(F.box).innerHTML = tagEditorHtml(tagTarget, field); if (refocus && $(F.input)) $(F.input).focus(); } function addTag(field, text) { const F = TAG_FIELDS[field], t = cleanTag(text), cur = editingTags(field); if (!t) return; if (t.length > TAG_MAX_CHARS) return toast(`Up to ${TAG_MAX_CHARS} characters`, true); if (cur.some(x => tagKey(x) === tagKey(t))) { $(F.input).value = ''; return toast(`That ${F.one} is already on this ${tagNoun(tagKind(), 1)}`); } if (cur.length >= TAG_MAX_COUNT) return toast(`Up to ${TAG_MAX_COUNT} ${F.label.toLowerCase()} each`, true); const existing = tagCounts(field)[tagKey(t)]; setTags(field, cur.concat(existing ? existing.label : t), true); } function removeTag(field, i) { const cur = editingTags(field).slice(); cur.splice(i, 1); setTags(field, cur); } function setTags(field, list, refocus) { if (viewOnly()) return; const h = tagHolder(); if (!h) return; if (!tagTarget) { h[field] = list; return renderTagBox(field, refocus); } const isItem = tagKind() === 'item', before = h[field]; h[field] = list; renderTagBox(field, refocus); render(); tagSaving = tagSaving.then(() => isItem ? api('setItemTags', h.id, h.key, list, field) : api('setOrderTags', h.orderNo, list, field)) .then(saved => { if (!isItem) Object.assign(h, saved, { tags: h.tags, lines: h.lines, endpoints: h.endpoints }); }) .catch(e => { h[field] = before; renderTagBox(field); render(); fail(e); }); } function unionTags(lists) { const seen = new Set(), out = []; lists.forEach(list => (list || []).forEach(t => { if (!seen.has(tagKey(t))) { seen.add(tagKey(t)); out.push(t); } })); return out; } function syncItemEndpoints(key) { const lines = orders.flatMap(o => o.lines.filter(l => l.key === key)); items.forEach(it => { if (it.key !== key) return; const eps = unionTags(lines.map(l => l.endpoints).concat([it.rcvEndpoints])), sns = unionTags(lines.map(l => (l.serials || []).filter(Boolean)).concat([it.rcvSerials])); Object.assign(it, { endpoints: eps, serials: sns }); }); } const epIcon = ck => ck === 'evse' ? '🔌' : '📦'; const epIconIn = (lines, ek) => epIcon(lines.some(l => lineCatKey(l) === 'evse' && (l.endpoints || []).some(e => tagKey(e) === ek)) ? 'evse' : ''); const lineHolder = scope => scope === 'od' ? od : scope === 'rcv' ? rcv : orderByNo(scope); const lineOf = (scope, i) => { const h = lineHolder(scope); return h && h.lines[i]; }; const lineEps = (scope, i) => { const l = lineOf(scope, i); return (l && l.endpoints) || []; }; const lineSns = (scope, i) => { const l = lineOf(scope, i); return lineEps(scope, i).map((_, j) => (l.serials || [])[j] || ''); }; const SERIAL_MAX = 60; const EP_INFO = [['rotary', 'Rotary dial', 10, 'e.g. 3', 'text'], ['amps', 'Amp draw', 10, 'A', 'decimal'], ['breaker', 'Breaker size', 10, 'A', 'decimal']]; const EP_COLS = ['serials', 'rotary', 'amps', 'breaker', 'share']; const epData = l => { const eps = (l && l.endpoints) || [], d = { endpoints: eps.slice() }; EP_COLS.forEach(c => d[c] = eps.map((_, j) => String(((l || {})[c] || [])[j] || ''))); return d; }; const ampText = v => /^\d+(\.\d+)?$/.test(v) ? v + ' A' : v; function epSummary(l, j) { const d = epData(l), v = c => d[c][j]; return [v('serials') && 'S/N ' + v('serials'), v('rotary') && 'Dial ' + v('rotary'), v('amps') && ampText(v('amps')) + ' draw', v('breaker') && ampText(v('breaker')) + ' breaker', v('share') && '⇄ ' + v('share')].filter(Boolean).join(' · '); } function shareChoices(h, name) { const all = h.lines.flatMap(l => (l.endpoints || []).map((e, j) => ({ e, share: epData(l).share[j] }))); const mine = (all.find(x => tagKey(x.e) === tagKey(name)) || {}).share || ''; const taken = new Set(all.filter(x => tagKey(x.e) !== tagKey(name) && x.share).map(x => tagKey(x.share))); return all.map(x => x.e).filter(e => tagKey(e) !== tagKey(name) && (!taken.has(tagKey(e)) || tagKey(e) === tagKey(mine))); } function lineEpHtml(scope, i) { const noOwner = epNoOwner(scope); const eps = lineEps(scope, i), sns = lineSns(scope, i), edit = canEdit() && !(orderLock && scope === orderLock) && !noOwner, sc = esc(scope); const ck = lineCatKey(lineOf(scope, i)), evse = ck === 'evse', icon = epIcon(ck), l = lineOf(scope, i), d = epData(l), h = lineHolder(scope); if (!eps.length && (!edit || !evse)) return noOwner && evse && canEdit() ? `
    🔒 This order has no Created By, so endpoints can’t be added.
    ` : ''; const x = (t, j) => ``; const mv = (t, j) => eps.length < 2 ? '' : ` `; const box = (t, j, c, label, max, ph, mode, id) => ``; const row = (t, j) => edit && !evse ? `${icon} ${esc(t)}${epSummary(l, j) ? ' · ' + esc(epSummary(l, j)) : ''}${x(t, j)}` : edit && scope === 'rcv' ? `
    ${icon} ${esc(t)} ${mv(t, j)}${x(t, j)}
    ` : edit ? `
    ${j + 1}${icon} ${esc(t)} ${mv(t, j)}${x(t, j)}
    ${box(t, j, 'serials', 'Serial #', SERIAL_MAX, 'Serial #', 'text', `lep_sn_${i}_${j}`).replace('class="ep-f"', 'class="ep-f wide"')} ${EP_INFO.map(([c, label, max, ph, mode]) => box(t, j, c, label, max, ph, mode)).join('')}
    ` : `${icon} ${esc(t)}${epSummary(l, j) ? ' · ' + esc(epSummary(l, j)) : ''}`; return `
    ${edit && evse && eps.length > 2 ? `
    ${eps.length} endpoints · ↑ ↓ to arrange
    ` : ''} ${eps.length ? (edit && evse ? eps.map(row).join('') : `
    ${eps.map(row).join('')}
    `) : ''} ${edit && !evse ? '
    Endpoints can only be added to EVSE items.
    ' : ''} ${noOwner && evse && canEdit() ? '
    🔒 This order has no Created By, so its endpoints can’t be changed.
    ' : ''} ${edit && evse && eps.length < TAG_MAX_COUNT ? `
    ` : ''}
    `; } const epAccOpen = new Set(); const lepRowHtml = (no, i) => { const h = lineEpHtml(no, i); if (!h) return ''; const eps = lineEps(no, i), sns = lineSns(no, i), filled = eps.filter((e, j) => String(sns[j] || '').trim()).length, open = !eps.length || epAccOpen.has(no + '|' + i); return `
    🔌 ${eps.length ? `${eps.length} endpoint${eps.length === 1 ? '' : 's'}${filled}/${eps.length} serial #` : 'Endpointsnone yet'}${eps.length ? `${esc(eps.slice(0, 4).join(' · '))}${eps.length > 4 ? ' …' : ''}` : ''}›
    ${h}
    `; }; function epAccToggle(el) { const k = el.dataset.k; el.open ? epAccOpen.add(k) : epAccOpen.delete(k); } function epAccAll(no, open) { document.querySelectorAll('.lep-acc').forEach(d => { d.open = open; open ? epAccOpen.add(d.dataset.k) : epAccOpen.delete(d.dataset.k); }); } function endpointUsedAt(name, scope, i) { const k = tagKey(name), has = l => (l.endpoints || []).some(e => tagKey(e) === k); const skipOrder = scope === 'od' ? od && od.orderNo : null; for (const o of orders) { if (o.status === 'Cancelled' || (skipOrder && o.orderNo === skipOrder)) continue; const j = o.lines.findIndex((l, n) => !(o.orderNo === scope && n === i) && has(l)); if (j >= 0) return `${o.orderNo} (${o.lines[j].name})`; } for (const h of [od && ['od', od], rcv && ['rcv', rcv]].filter(Boolean)) { const j = h[1].lines.findIndex((l, n) => !(h[0] === scope && n === i) && has(l)); if (j >= 0) { const it = items.find(x => x.key === h[1].lines[j].key); return `${h[0] === 'od' ? 'this order' : 'this receipt'} (${it ? it.name : 'another item'})`; } } const it = items.find(x => (x.rcvEndpoints || []).some(e => tagKey(e) === k)); return it ? `a receipt (${it.name})` : ''; } function renderLineEp(scope, i, focus) { if (!$('lep_' + i)) return; $('lep_' + i).innerHTML = lineEpHtml(scope, i); showEpStatus(scope); const el = focus === 'add' ? $('lep_in_' + i) : focus != null ? $(`lep_sn_${i}_${focus}`) : null; if (el) el.focus(); } function addLineEp(scope, i, text) { const t = cleanTag(text), cur = lineEps(scope, i); if (!t) return; if (lineCatKey(lineOf(scope, i)) !== 'evse') return toast('Endpoints can only be added to EVSE items', true); if (t.length > TAG_MAX_CHARS) return toast(`Up to ${TAG_MAX_CHARS} characters`, true); if (cur.some(x => tagKey(x) === tagKey(t))) { $('lep_in_' + i).value = ''; return toast('That endpoint is already on this item'); } if (cur.length >= TAG_MAX_COUNT) return toast(`Up to ${TAG_MAX_COUNT} endpoints each`, true); const at = endpointUsedAt(t, scope, i); if (at) return toast(`${t} is already used on ${at}. SERIAL_MAX : 10; const label = col === 'serials' ? 'Serial numbers' : (EP_INFO.find(f => f[0] === col) || [, 'That'])[1]; if (v.length > max) return toast(`${label} can be up to ${max} characters`, true); editLineEps(scope, h => { if (h.lines[i][col][j] === v) return false; h.lines[i][col][j] = v; }, { quiet: true }); flushEpSave(scope); } function typeLineInfo(scope, i, j, col, value) { const v = cleanTag(value); if (v.length > (col === 'serials' ? od && od.orderNo && orderByNo(od.orderNo) : scope !== 'rcv' && orderByNo(scope); return !!o && !String(o.createdBy || '').trim(); } function editLineEps(scope, fn, opts = {}) { if (viewOnly()) return; if (epNoOwner(scope)) return toast('🔒 This order has no Created By, so its endpoints can’t be changed.', true); const h = lineHolder(scope); if (!h) return; const before = h.lines.map(epData), was = before.map(d => JSON.stringify(d)); h.lines.forEach(l => Object.assign(l, epData(l))); if (fn(h) === false) return; const changed = h.lines.map((l, n) => JSON.stringify(epData(l)) !== was[n] ? 'saved' : 'dirty'; }); } catch (e) { const cur = orderByNo(no) || h; idx.forEach(n => { if (cur.lines[n] && q.good[n]) Object.assign(cur.lines[n], q.good[n]); q.lines.delete(n); }); Object.keys(q.field).forEach(f => { if (idx.includes(+f.split('|')[0])) q.field[f] = 'error'; }); if ($('sheet').dataset.order === no) cur.lines.forEach((_, n) => renderLineEp(no, n)); epResync(cur); fail(e); } finally { q.running = false; showEpStatus(no); if (q.lines.size) runEpSave(no); setTimeout(() => { Object.keys(q.field).forEach(f => { if (q.field[f] === 'saved' && q.ver[f] === (vers[f] || q.ver[f])) delete q.field[f]; }); showEpStatus(no); }, 2600); } } function flushEpSave(no) { const q = epSaves[no]; if (q && q.lines.size) { clearTimeout(q.timer); runEpSave(no); } } function showEpStatus(no) { const q = epSaves[no]; document.querySelectorAll('#sheet .ep-f[data-f]').forEach(el => { const [o, ...rest] = el.dataset.f.split('|'); if (o !== no) return; const st = (q && q.field[rest.join('|')]) || ''; el.classList.remove('f-dirty', 'f-saving', 'f-saved', 'f-error'); if (st) el.classList.add('f-' + st); }); } function keepLocalEps(fresh) { return fresh.map(o => { const old = epBusy(o.orderNo) && orderByNo(o.orderNo); return old ? Object.assign(o, { lines: old.lines, endpoints: old.endpoints }) : o; }); } document.addEventListener('visibilitychange', () => { if (document.visibilityState === 'hidden') Object.keys(epSaves).forEach(no => { clearTimeout(epSaves[no].timer); runEpSave(no); }); }); function itemEpHtml(it) { const eps = it.endpoints || []; if (!eps.length) return ''; const uses = orders.flatMap(o => o.lines.filter(l => l.key === it.key && (l.endpoints || []).length).map(l => ({ o, l }))); const epText = l => l.endpoints.map((p, j) => p + (epSummary(l, j) ? ` (${epSummary(l, j)})` : '')).join('; '); return `
    Endpoints · ${eps.length}

    ${uses.map(u => ``).join('')}
    Endpoints and serial #s are added on this item inside each order, or when it's received (see Stock history).
    `; } let epPopFor = null, epPopSticky = false, epPopT = null, epPopTries = 0; const canHover = () => window.matchMedia && matchMedia('(hover: hover) and (pointer: fine)').matches; function epPopHtml(itemKey, ep) { const k = tagKey(ep), has = l => l.key === itemKey && (l.endpoints || []).some(e => tagKey(e) === k); const uses = orders.flatMap(o => o.lines.filter(has).map(l => ({ o, l, j: l.endpoints.findIndex(e => tagKey(e) === k) }))) .sort((a, b) => (a.o.status === 'Cancelled') - (b.o.status === 'Cancelled')); const rows = uses.map(({ o, l, j }) => { const d = epData(l), v = c => d[c][j]; const more = [v('breaker') && ampText(v('breaker')) + ' breaker', v('rotary') && 'Dial ' + v('rotary'), v('serials') && 'S/N ' + v('serials'), v('share') && '⇄ ' + v('share')].filter(Boolean); return `

    ${esc(more.join(' · '))}
    ` : ''}

    `; }); const loaded = !canSee('stock') || searchRcpts.return; [['return', '↩️', 'Returned'], ['po', '📥', 'Received on a PO']].forEach(([kind, ic, word]) => (searchRcpts[kind] || []).forEach(r => { if (!r.lines.some(has)) return; const what = kind === 'po' ?     `); })); return `

    ${rows.join('') || (loaded ? '
    Not on any order or receipt yet.
    ' : '')} ${loaded ? '' : `
    ${epPopTries > 1 ? 'Returns and POs couldn’t be checked right now.' : 'Checking returns and POs…'}
    `}`; } function epPopShow(chip, sticky) { clearTimeout(epPopT); const sh = $('sheet'); if (!chip || !sh.contains(chip)) return; let pop = $('epPop'); if (!pop) { pop = document.createElement('div'); } if (pop.parentNode !== sh) sh.appendChild(pop); document.querySelectorAll('.iep.open').forEach(c => c !== chip && c.classList.remove('open')); chip.classList.add('open'); epPopFor = chip; epPopSticky = !!sticky;     No other items match.

Saved to 📂 Saved catalogs'); } catch (e) { fail(e); } if ($('catPdf')) { $('catPdf').disabled = false; $('catPdf').textContent = '📄 Make PDF again'; } } const CH_PAL = { light: ['#2a78d6', '#eb6834', '#1baf7a', '#eda100', '#e87ba4', '#008300', '#6250d6', '#e34948'], dark: ['#3987e5', '#d95926', '#199e70', '#c98500', '#d55181', '#008300', '#9085e9', '#e66767'] }; const CH_OTHER = '#8a8f98'; const CH_SVGNS = 'http:' + '\x2F\x2F' + 'www.w3.org/2000/svg'; const CH_DATA = { orders: { label: '🚚 Orders', by: [['month', 'Month'], ['week', 'Week'], ['status', 'Status'], ['project', 'Project'], ['pm', 'PM'], ['vendor', 'Company / vendor'], ['activity', 'Activity type'], ['to', 'To location']], measure: [['count', 'Number of orders'], ['units', 'Units'], ['cost', 'Our cost'], ['total', 'Total with shipping & additions']] }, items: { label: '📦 Items', by: [['category', 'Category'], ['location', 'Location'], ['shelf', 'Shelf'], ['job', 'Job'], ['manufacturer', 'Manufacturer']], measure: [['rows', 'Number of items'], ['units', 'Units on hand'], ['value', 'Value on hand']] }, }; const CH_TYPES = [['bar', '▮ Bars'], ['hbar', '▬ Sideways bars'], ['donut', '◔ Donut'], ['line', '📈 Line']]; const CH_RANGES = [['all', 'All time'], ['year', 'This year'], ['90', 'Last 90 days'], ['month', 'This month']]; const CH_STARTERS = [ { title: 'Orders by month', data: 'orders', by: 'month', measure: 'count', type: 'bar', range: 'year' }, { title: 'Order totals by project', data: 'orders', by: 'project', measure: 'total', type: 'hbar', range: 'all' }, { title: 'Inventory value by category', data: 'items', by: 'category', measure: 'value', type: 'donut' }, ]; const chTime = c => c.data === 'orders' && (c.by === 'month' || c.by === 'week'); const chMoney = c => ['cost', 'total', 'value'].includes(c.measure); const chLabelOf = (c, kind) => (CH_DATA[c.data][kind].find(x => x[0] === c[kind]) || ['', ''])[1]; const chTitle = c => (c.title || '').trim() || `${chLabelOf(c, 'measure')} by ${chLabelOf(c, 'by').toLowerCase()}`; let myCharts = (() => { try { const v = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(emoKey('charts'))); } }; function chartRows(c) { const fmtV = v => chMoney(c) ? money(n0(v)) : Math.round(v).toLocaleString(); if (c.data === 'items') { const ext = i => i.extended != null ? Number(i.extended) || 0 : (Number(i.cost) || 0) * Math.max(0, Number(i.quantity) || 0); const key = { category: i => i.category || '(no category)', location: i => i.location || '(no location)', shelf: i => [i.location, i.shelf && 'shelf ' + i.shelf].filter(Boolean).join(' · ') || '(no shelf)', job: i => jobOf(i.job) || 'Available', manufacturer: i => i.manufacturer || '(no manufacturer)' }[c.by]; const val = { rows: () => 1, units: i => Math.max(0, Number(i.quantity) || 0), value: ext }[c.measure]; return chFold([...groupBy(items, key)].map(([label, l]) => ({ label: String(label), v: l.reduce((n, i) => n + val(i), 0), n: l.length })), fmtV, 'item'); } const today = new Date(), from = { all: '', year: today.getFullYear() + '-01-01', month: dayKey(today).slice(0, 8) + '01', '90': dayKey(new Date(today.getTime() - 89 * 864e5)) }[c.range || 'all'] || ''; const list = orders.filter(o => (c.cancelled || o.status !== 'Cancelled') && (!from || (dayKeyOf(o.orderDate) || '') >= from) && (!chTime(c) || dayKeyOf(o.orderDate))); const val = { count: () => 1, units: linesUnits, cost: linesCost, total: orderTotal }[c.measure]; if (chTime(c)) { const wk = k => { const d = new Date(k + 'T12:00:00'); d.setDate(d.getDate() - ((d.getDay() + 6) % 7)); return dayKey(d); }; const bucket = c.by === 'month' ? k => k.slice(0, 7) : wk; const g = groupBy(list, o => bucket(dayKeyOf(o.orderDate))); const keys = [...g.keys()].sort(); if (!keys.length) return { rows: [], fmtV }; const out = [], last = from ? bucket(dayKey(today)) : keys[keys.length - 1]; let k = from ? bucket(from) : keys[0]; for (let guard = 0; k <= last && guard < 400; guard++) { const l = g.get(k) || []; out.push({ label: c.by === 'month' ? new Date(k + '-15T12:00:00').toLocaleDateString([], { month: 'short', year: '2-digit' }) : new Date(k + 'T12:00:00').toLocaleDateString([], { month: 'short', day: 'numeric' }), long: c.by === 'month' ? new Date(k + '-15T12:00:00').toLocaleDateString([], { month: 'long', year: 'numeric' }) : 'Week of ' + fmtDay(k), v: l.reduce((n, o) => n + val(o), 0), n: l.length }); if (c.by === 'month') { const d = new Date(k + '-15T12:00:00'); d.setMonth(d.getMonth() + 1); k = dayKey(d).slice(0, 7); } else k = dayKey(new Date(new Date(k + 'T12:00:00').getTime() + 7 * 864e5)); } const cap = c.by === 'month' ? 24 : 26; return { rows: out.slice(-cap), fmtV, note: out.length > cap ? `The last ${cap} ${c.by}s.` : '' }; } const key = { status: o => o.status, project: o => o.projectName || '(no project)', pm: o => o.pm || '(no PM)', vendor: o => o.vendor || '(no company)', activity: o => o.activityType || '(no activity type)', to: o => o.toLocation || '(no location)' }[c.by]; return chFold([...groupBy(list, key)].map(([label, l]) => ({ label: String(label), v: l.reduce((n, o) => n + val(o), 0), n: l.length })), fmtV, 'order'); } function chFold(rows, fmtV, noun) { rows = rows.filter(r => r.v > 0).sort((a, b) => b.v - a.v || a.label.localeCompare(b.label)); if (rows.length > 8) { const rest = rows.slice(7); rows = rows.slice(0, 7).concat({ label: `Other (${rest.length})`, v: rest.reduce((n, r) => n + r.v, 0), n: rest.reduce((n, r) => n + r.n, 0), other: true, parts: rest }); } return { rows, fmtV, noun }; } const chShort = v => Math.abs(v) >= 1e6 ? (v / 1e6).toFixed(v >= 1e7 ? 0 : 1).replace(/\.0$/, '') + 'M' : Math.abs(v) >= 1e3 ? (v / 1e3).toFixed(v >= 1e4 ? 0 : 1).replace(/\.0$/, '') + 'k' : String(Math.round(v * 100) / 100); const chTick = (c, v) => (chMoney(c) ? '$' : '') + chShort(v); function chNice(max) { if (max <= 0) return { top: 1, step: 1 }; const raw = max / 4, mag = Math.pow(10, Math.floor(Math.log10(raw))), f = raw / mag, step = (f <= 1 ? 1 : f <= 2 ? 2 : f <= 2.5 ? 2.5 : f <= 5 ? 5 : 10) * mag; return { top: Math.ceil(max / step) * step, step }; } const chFit = (s, n) => s.length > n ? s.slice(0, Math.max(1, n - 1)) + '…' : s; function chColors(el) { const cs = getComputedStyle(el || document.body), v = k => (cs.getPropertyValue(k) || '').trim(); const probe = document.createElement('i'); probe.style.color = v('--card') || '#fff'; document.body.appendChild(probe); const m = /(\d+)\D+(\d+)\D+(\d+)/.exec(getComputedStyle(probe).color) || [0, 255, 255, 255]; probe.remove(); const lum = (+m[1] * 299 + +m[2] * 587 + +m[3] * 114) / 1000, dark = lum < 128; return { dark, pal: CH_PAL[dark ? 'dark' : 'light'], card: v('--card') || '#ffffff', text: v('--text') || '#1f2937', muted: v('--muted') || '#6b7280', line: v('--line') || '#e5e7eb', font: cs.fontFamily || 'system-ui, sans-serif' }; } function chartSvg(c, W, col, opts = {}) { const d = chartRows(c), rows = d.rows, total = rows.reduce((n, r) => n + r.v, 0); const top0 = opts.title ? 34 : 0; const tipOf = r => `${r.long || r.label}\n${d.fmtV(r.v)}${c.type === 'donut' && total ?
